# Numbers in the paper and where each comes from

One row per number in `paper/main.tex` and `paper/sections/*.tex` (abstract, body, figure and table captions, the statements after the conclusion, appendix). A value used in several places has one row that lists every place. "Where" uses section numbers of the built PDF (Abs = abstract, Avail / AI / Ack = the unnumbered "Code and data availability", "Use of AI tools" and "Acknowledgments" statements, A-G = appendices) and names figures and tables by their labels (F-e2 = `fig:e2`, T-fpval = `tab:fpval`, ...), so that a row does not change when a float moves. "Derived" means arithmetic on committed values; the arithmetic is given. Paths are relative to the repository root and, except those marked outside the repository or unmerged, resolve there (checked on 2026-10-31; `COORD/COORDINATION.md` is the coordination file outside the repository and is not public, and `notes/STATE_llama31.md` is on the unmerged branch r12/llama31; re-run the path check after that merge and keep this sentence only if it passes). A run that an analysis, a `meta.json` or a derived CSV names by its cluster path `results/agentload_*/<ts>` is stored under the pull directory of its jobs with the same final components, `results/nova/<pull>/results/agentload_*/<ts>` (the Stage 0, Stage C and E0a pulls have no inner `results/`: `results/nova/<pull>/agentload_*/<ts>`); the derived CSVs give both (`run_dir`, `run_path`). Commits are on `main` (or its ancestors) unless noted; the files of the 2026-10-30 revision that this file once marked "uncommitted" are committed on branch `r10/arxiv` (the extended `paper/derived/` files in baeae81, `provenance/` except `commit-map.tsv` in 3c755f3); job IDs are SLURM IDs on Nova; times are CDT (UTC-5). Section numbers are those of fix-up 1 of fill 2 (2026-10-31); the subsection "Fix-up 1 of fill 2" near the end lists them and the conversion applied to every row. The list that follows is the earlier fill-2 numbering, kept as the record of the previous conversions: 1 Introduction, 2 Background, 3 Quasi-static model (3.1-3.4), 4 Methodology (4.1-4.4), 5 Results (5.1 curve and edge, 5.2 paired tests incl. native and vLLM, 5.3 H200, 5.4 sustaining variable, 5.5 rescue, 5.6 basin, 5.7 other orderings), 6 First-principles model (6.1 model, 6.2 results incl. Proposition E, 6.3 heuristics, 6.4 validation, 6.5 weaknesses, 6.6 prior models), 7 Frozen predictions, 8 Diagnostic, 9 Related work, 10 Limitations, 11 Conclusion; Appendices A protocol, B registrations, C prediction rounds, timeline and blind simulator predictions, D provenance, E proofs of the quasi-static results, F first-principles model, G supplementary results (G.1-G.9), H frozen predictions in full, I simulator studies; tables T-e2, T-gapR, T-rescue, T-fpval, T-frozensum (main), T-stages, T-workload, T-rule, T-ledger, T-timeline, T-timeline-vllm, T-timeline-r11, T-blind, T-prov, T-propE, T-rescueruns, T-frozen, T-frozen2; figures F-e2, F-curve, F-rescue, F-fpmu (main), F-fixed, F-edge, F-e2native, F-grid, F-fpscale, F-robust, F-mech. The "Where" entries of the rows written before 2026-10-31 were converted from the 2026-10-30 numbering (old 4.x to 5.x; 5.1-5.4 to 6.1-6.4; 5.5 to 6.7; 5.6 to 6.8; 5.7 to 6.5; 6 to 8.1; 7 to 8.2; 8-11 to 9-12; F1-F7 to F-e2, F-curve, F-edge, F-e2native, F-grid, F-robust, F-mech; T1-T8 to T-e2, T-blind, T-stages, T-workload, T-rule, T-timeline, T-timeline-vllm, T-prov), and rows whose sentences the revision moves to Appendix E (the (A3)/(A4) checks and the pin numbers of 3.4) or Appendix G (the B1n-B4n round, the LPM fallback analysis, the trace and mechanism details) name E or G; check them against the built PDF. On 2026-10-31 (fill 2) the "Where" entries of every row written before fill 2 were converted again, from the 2026-10-31 numbering to the fill-2 numbering above (old 4.1-4.6 to 6.1-6.6; 5.1-5.4 to 4.1-4.4; 6.1-6.2 to 5.1; 6.3-6.5 to 5.2; 6.6 to 5.4; 6.7 to C and 7; 6.8 to 5.7; 7 to 7 and H; 8.1, 8.2 to I.1, I.2; 9-12 to 8-11). Where a section's text was split between the main text and an appendix (3.3 and 3.4 to E and A; 4.4-4.6 to H and F; 5.3, 5.4 to B, C; 6.1-6.5 and 6.8 to G.1, G.2, G.4 and the drain times to A; 9, 10, 11 to G.7, G.9, G.8 and the registration slips to C; the availability statement to D), the place was chosen by searching the row's values in the new text of each candidate (`remap_where.py`, kept outside the repository with the fill-2 notes), and both places are listed when the search could not decide; check them against the built PDF. The paper's `\pending{...}` items are the outcomes of the frozen predictions of Section 7 that have not arrived (Tables `tab:frozen` and `tab:frozen2`), the pending cells of the stage table and the registration ledger (Appendices A and B), the refreshed accounting export and the public repository's URL (`\repourl`); each is listed under "Pending" at the end. Every number added or changed in the 2026-10-30 and 2026-10-31 revisions was recomputed for this file from the primary run files (pandas), independently of the editors' scripts and of `figures/paper/derived_numbers.py`; where a derived CSV holds the same value it is cited as well.

## Fill 1 (2026-10-31: H200 300k, B1, E0F, LPM-INF, pred2; Sections Abs, 1, 6-7, 11, A-C, G; Tables T-frozen, T-frozen2, T-stages, T-ledger, T-timeline-r11, T-prov)

The subsection "Fill 1, editor frozen_theory" above holds the Section 7 rows of this round. The rows below are an independent recomputation from the run files and cover every number of the PAPER_INPUT notes for H200, B1/E0F and LPM-INF that the paper may use, plus the appendix tables; where both subsections list a value they agree (checked value by value on 2026-10-31). The branches `r11/strengthen`, `r11/lpminf`, `r11/rescue`, `r12/h200`, `r12/llama31-predict`, `r13/pred2` and `r14/extra` are merged into `r10/arxiv` at cdb9a99, so every path below resolves in the working tree, and the **(unmerged)** marks in the rows above are superseded, except those for `notes/STATE_llama31.md` (branch r12/llama31, still unmerged) (the files at the tags are unchanged: `git diff cfa7712 HEAD -- results/theory/pred2 configs/r13_theory_pred2.yaml` and `git diff 997d2c4 HEAD -- results/theory/merged` are empty). Every value below was recomputed on 2026-10-31 by `recompute_fill1.py` (kept outside the repository with the fill notes; it reads only the files cited and writes nothing into the repository): closed-curve values from each run's `requests.csv` and `sessions.csv` over [900, 2700) s (hit = ok turns completing in the window with cached/prompt > 0.5; mu turns-based = those turns / 1800 s / E[T] = 14.761; mu sessions = sessions ending in the window / 1800 s), B1 and LPM-INF hit fractions from `requests.csv` over [5700, 8700) s, realized arrival rates from `sessions.csv` (base sessions arriving in [0, 3000) s / 3000 s / lambda_ref 0.0715340); every recomputed value equals the committed analysis output it is checked against. Push times were read from GitHub's activity log (`gh api repos/AkshatValse/Nova-Research/activity`) at 06:52 CDT on 2026-10-31 (UTC - 5 h); job times from `sacct` on Nova the same morning; run end times from each run's `meta.json`. Keys: HP = `results/nova/h200_wave1_pulled_20261031-023416/` (d108056), HP2 = `results/nova/h200_stageC_extra_1716961x_pulled_20261031-040933/` (748900b), HP3 = `results/nova/h200_e0b_reseeds_1716966x_pulled_20261031-050308/` (4e27364), HRC = `prereg/inputs/2026-10-31-h200/fill_rulecheck_INCOMPLETE.yaml` (d108056), BP = `results/nova/b1_e0f_1716850x_pulled_20261031-040517/` (4dd21e0), LP = `results/nova/lpminf_1716874x_pulled_20261031-042548/` (fc8b028), LE = `results/r11_lpminf_eval/20261031-eval/runs.csv` (fc8b028), S1 = `results/r11_predict_basin/20261030-194539/{basin_runs.csv, e0b_runs.csv}` (c600fd7; mixed mode, seeds 0-2 or 3-5), P2 = `results/theory/pred2/*.json` (cfa7712), ACT = the activity log as read above.

## Fix-up 1 of fill 2 (2026-10-31)

Section numbers after this fix-up: 1 Introduction, 2 Background (engine source lines moved to E; the full "what is classical" paragraph to G.9), 3 A fluid model of the open system (3.1 workload, 3.2 thrashing curve, 3.3 quasi-static approximation, 3.4 caps and a congestion bound; Proposition A and its remark moved to E), 4 Methodology (4.1-4.4; the stage sequence moved to A, the registration push times to B), 5 Results (5.1 curve and edge, 5.2 paired tests, 5.3 rescue, 5.4 basin, 5.5 H200, 5.6 other orderings), 6 First-principles model (6.1 model, 6.2 results, 6.3 heuristics, 6.4 sustaining variable, 6.5 validation, 6.6 weaknesses, 6.7 prior models), 7-11 unchanged. Every "Where" cell above was converted from the fill-2 numbering by `fix1/ed_numbers.py` (scratchpad, outside the repository): 5.3 to 5.5, 5.4 to 6.4, 5.5 to 5.3, 5.6 to 5.4, 5.7 to 5.6, 6.4 to 6.5, 6.5 to 6.6, 6.6 to 6.7, outside parentheses only; rows whose sentences left 2, 3.2-3.4 or 4.3 were edited by hand (listed in the script). The paper now calls the basin tests BASIN-1 and BASIN-2; this file and the repository keep their registration names B1 and B2 (`b25`, `b100`, `b40`, `b50`, `b60`), and B1-B4, B1n-B4n, B1v-B4v remain the simulator prediction rounds of Appendix C. F-fpmu has a fourth panel (H200, frozen prediction against MU12; the values are the rows "H200 closed curve" and "H200 primary predictions" above) and F-fpscale (a) plots the measured H200 peak ratio (row "1.89 (1.8940); 1.99 (1.9915)"). Proposition numbers in the built PDF are now: 1 cap identity (Sec. 3.4), 2 stationary states and all-miss onset, 3 speed law, 4 exogenous gaps ("Proposition E"), 5 fold identity, 6 trapped identity (Sec. 6), 7 request-level FCFS bound ("Proposition A", Appendix E); rows above that name a proposition by number use the numbering of the build in which they were written.
